Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents
Agensh：将组织智能扩展至1,024个智能体
A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.
中文翻译
多智能体系统可通过并发执行任务来降低复杂任务的延迟。尽管已有若干开创性的多智能体框架支持多智能体系统，但当前多智能体框架的可扩展性常受制于中央协调器处理任务分配和工作协调的能力。为解决这一限制，我们引入Agensh，一种无中央协调器的可扩展自组织多智能体框架：并发工作者执行多智能体协作循环，异步地持续收集上下文、申领并自主分配子任务、采取行动、共享发现、验证结果，并合并进展。该循环由智能体组织基础设施支持，包含三个组件：共享工作区存放待定、进行中和已完成的工作；消息接口使工作者之间通信；共享上下文保留可重用的发现和工作意图。为测试Agensh的可扩展性，我们在ProgramBench中最难的五个任务上使用GPT-5.6-sol（高）进行评估。从1个智能体扩展到128个智能体，平均最终测试通过率从19.31%提升至28.78%，相对提升约49%。更大的组织更早达到相近的测试通过率。在pandoc任务上，从1个智能体扩展到1,024个智能体，最终测试通过率从33.89%提升至55.06%。工作者轨迹分析显示，随着组织规模扩大，不同形式的自组织协作逐渐涌现并趋于标准化。这些结果揭示了智能体数量作为一个新的可扩展维度，使多智能体组织能够拓展通用智能的边界，为在严格延迟约束或时间预算下完成复杂任务提供了实用解决方案。
👤 Zhihao Zhan, Ting Song, Li Dong, Shaohan Huang, Jianxun Lian et al. 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Measuring the Serving Stack Instead of the Model: Hidden Confounds in Local Tool-Use Evaluation
衡量服务栈而非模型本身：本地工具使用评估中的隐藏混淆因素
A coding agent must emit a valid tool call--a parseable invocation of a tool in the provided schema--before the harness can execute its chosen action. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. In Ollama, the default tools= request is gated per model by a static template flag: some models are accepted and return calls as text, some return native tool_calls, while Phi-3 and Gemma-3 are rejected before inference. In our harness, rejection and retry exhaustion are not preserved as structured failure metadata, so downstream analysis can misclassify them as model non-calls and naively report 0% fidelity. Adding a text tool list while retaining the native channel recovers much of the measured fidelity for accepted models, whereas a uniform text protocol reduces fidelity for Llama-3.2, which has native tool-call support. Cross-stack probes on Ollama, this http URL , vLLM, and SGLang show different handling of the same request. Constrained decoding removes parse failures but can induce non-termination, and turn-pooled versus per-instance estimates differ by up to about 55 points. We conclude with a checklist for treating serving behavior as part of the evaluation protocol.
中文翻译
一个编码智能体必须在调度系统执行其选择的操作前发出有效工具调用——即根据提供的模式解析的工具调用。我们研究本地服务栈如何影响这一协议步骤，并表明测量结果可能依赖于服务层而非仅模型行为。在Ollama中，默认的tools=请求由每个模型的静态模板标志控制：部分模型被接受并返回文本形式的调用，部分返回原生tool_calls，而Phi-3和Gemma-3则在推理前被拒绝。在我们的调度系统中，拒绝和重试耗尽未被保留为结构化失败元数据，导致下游分析可能将其误判为模型未调用，并天真地报告0%的保真度。在保留原生通道的同时添加文本工具列表，可恢复被接受模型的大部分测量保真度；而统一使用文本协议则降低了具有原生工具调用支持的Llama-3.2的保真度。对Ollama、this http URL、vLLM和SGLang的跨栈探测显示，它们对同一请求的处理方式不同。约束解码虽可消除解析失败，但可能导致非终止，而按轮次聚合与按实例估计的差异可达约55个百分点。最后，我们提供一份检查清单，建议将服务行为视为评估协议的一部分。
👤 Lijuan Tang, Yuemeng Zheng 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

PERSONAWEAVER: Controllable Diversity Beyond Conventional Archetypes in Procedural Character Generation
PERSONAWEAVER：超越传统原型的程序化角色生成中的可控多样性
Procedural character generation aims to populate games, simulations, and other virtual worlds with diverse characters. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for scaling this task. However, LLM-based procedural character generation remains at an early stage: existing methods either generate characters directly or adapt profiles retrieved from persona banks. As we show, both approaches produce behaviorally homogeneous populations: characters overwhelmingly agree with positive moral norms and respond to questions with helpful, assistant-like reactions. To mitigate this homogenization, we introduce PersonaWeaver, which disentangles world building from behavioral specification and models behavior through setting general, diverse, manually curated banks of moral positions and conversational reactions. This design allows us to test how far LLM(s) can be pushed beyond their default behavioral patterns across settings. Across ten realistic and fantastical settings and three LLM(s), PersonaWeaver produces broader moral and interactional response distributions than prior work. Its guidance also diversifies interpersonal language, response length, and sentiment. It also produces less archetypal combinations of world attributes. Code is available at this https URL .
中文翻译
程序化角色生成旨在为游戏、模拟及其他虚拟世界填充多样化角色。大型语言模型（LLMs）为该任务的规模化提供了有前景的基础。然而，基于LLM的角色生成仍处于早期阶段：现有方法要么直接生成角色，要么改编从角色库中检索的档案。如我们所展示，这两种方法均产生行为同质化的群体：角色普遍符合积极道德规范，对问题回应以帮助性、助手式反应为主。为缓解这种同质化，我们引入PersonaWeaver，它将世界观构建与行为设定解耦，并通过设置通用、多样、人工精心策划的道德立场和对话反应库来建模行为。该设计使我们能够测试LLM在不同情境下能多大程度突破其默认行为模式。在十个现实与奇幻场景及三种LLM上，PersonaWeaver生成的道德和互动反应分布比以往工作更广泛。其引导机制也丰富了人际语言、响应长度和情感表达，生成更少典型组合的世界属性。
👤 Maan Qraitem, Kate Saenko, Bryan A. Plummer 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud
能够自言自语的语音语言模型
While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.
中文翻译
虽然思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）推理提升了语言模型的能力，但直接将其应用于语音语言模型（SLMs）可能在串行的“先思考再说话”范式下引入长时间的沉默间隔，破坏实时语音交互。为解决此问题，我们提出一种异步自言自语框架，用于基于推理的语音语言模型，该框架嵌入于思考者-说话者（Thinker-Talker）架构中。该框架维持一个主推理流用于逻辑推导，并引入一个轻量级的自言自语流，根据用户输入和不断演化的推理状态生成简短的任务相关进展话语。动态平衡策略在运行时协调两条流，当检测到即将出现沉默间隙时触发额外的自言自语语音，而在最终响应就绪时取消待发话语。在语音推理和问答基准上的实验表明，我们的方法显著减少了推理过程中的用户可听沉默时间，同时保持了与串行“先思考再说话”基线相当的答案准确性，展示了异步自言自语在语音语言模型中实现响应式交互的潜力。
👤 Junyi Ao, Kainan Peng, Mingbo Ma, Shun Zhang, Zhenyu Tang et al. 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Same Chart, Different Story: Bias in Vision-Language Chart Interpretation
同一图表，不同故事：视觉-语言图表解读中的偏见
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret charts and generate natural-language explanations for socially consequential data. However, they may produce different narratives for the same chart when only the referenced social group changes, reinforcing stereotypes and misleading decisions. Despite these risks, no benchmark exists for systematically evaluating bias in chart interpretation across social dimensions. We introduce ChartBias, the first benchmark for auditing bias in VLM-based chart interpretation. ChartBias contains 820 manually curated real-world charts spanning six attributes: race, income, age, religion, immigration status, and gender, yielding 4,319 valid chart, attribute instances and 8,638 paired generations where the chart is fixed and only the group term is swapped. Across 12 proprietary and open-source VLMs, totaling 155,484 model responses, we find three widespread failure modes: narrative shift (same chart, different narratives), group hallucination (assigning a chart to a group without evidence), and preference polarity (favourable trends often linked to one group). We further propose a multi-agent mitigation framework that serves as a strong baseline by separating chart-grounded evidence extraction from group-conditioned generation and using a counterfactual judge to verify that group-driven differences are supported by the chart. The framework substantially reduces narrative shift while preserving chart-grounded reasoning. Our findings show that evaluating chart understanding requires measuring not only accuracy, but also fairness and consistency across social groups. We release ChartBias at this https URL .
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）被广泛用于解释图表并生成自然语言描述，以应对具有社会意义的数据。然而，当仅参考的社会群体发生变化时，它们可能对同一图表生成不同的叙事，从而强化刻板印象并误导决策。尽管存在此类风险，目前尚无系统评估VLM在跨社会维度图表解读中偏见的基准。我们提出了ChartBias，首个用于审计VLM图表解读偏见的基准。ChartBias包含820个手工筛选的真实世界图表，涵盖种族、收入、年龄、宗教、移民身份和性别六个属性，共产生4,319个有效的图表-属性实例和8,638对成对生成内容（图表固定，仅群体术语替换）。在12个专有和开源VLM上，总计155,484条模型响应中，我们发现三种普遍失败模式：叙事转移（相同图表生成不同叙述）、群体幻觉（无证据地将图表归因于某群体）、偏好极性（有利趋势常与某一特定群体关联）。我们进一步提出一种多智能体缓解框架，通过分离基于图表的证据提取与群体条件生成，并利用反事实裁判验证群体驱动差异是否由图表支持，作为强基线。该框架显著减少了叙事转移，同时保持了基于图表的推理能力。研究揭示，评估图表理解不仅需关注准确性，还需衡量跨社会群体的公平性与一致性。我们已在该网址发布ChartBias。
👤 Mizanur Rahman, Huan Wu, Arash Asgari, Enamul Hoque Prince, Laleh Seyyed-Kalantari 📅 2026/08/12 🔗 arXiv

Beyond Static Charts: Can Language and Vision Language Models Generate Interactive Data Visualization Interfaces?
超越静态图表：语言与视觉-语言模型能否生成交互式数据可视化界面？
Data visualization is central to analytical reasoning, but real-world analysis increasingly requires language-driven interactive interfaces rather than static charts. Although recent large language and vision language models (LLMs/VLMs) have shown promise in generating static charts from natural language, their ability to generate interactive data visualization interfaces remains largely unexplored due to the lack of benchmarks. We introduce VIS-GEN, a benchmark for evaluating how well LLMs/VLMs can generate interactive visualization interfaces from natural language queries. VIS-GEN comprises 3,042 samples covering diverse analytical intents, including data filtering, temporal analysis, and visualization editing, each paired with dataset metadata and natural language queries that are designed to reflect realistic, goal driven data exploration scenarios. We benchmark 14 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source LLMs/VLMs, revealing large performance gaps and frequent failures on queries involving implicit intent, multiple interaction alternatives, and complex editing operations, highlighting interactive interface generation as a key open challenge beyond static chart synthesis. To address this, we propose a structured multi stage interface generation framework that decomposes the task into visualization design representation, generation of multiple interface candidates, constraint-aware critique, and self-refinement. This approach improves the best models pass rate by 15.9 percentage points, demonstrating a practical path toward more reliable language-driven interactive visualization systems. We release VIS-GEN at this https URL .
中文翻译
数据可视化是分析推理的核心，但现实分析日益需要由语言驱动的交互式界面，而非静态图表。尽管近期的大语言模型（LLMs）和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在从自然语言生成静态图表方面展现出潜力，但其生成交互式可视化界面的能力仍缺乏系统探索，主要受限于缺乏基准。我们引入VIS-GEN，一个用于评估LLMs/VLMs从自然语言查询生成交互式可视化界面效果的基准。VIS-GEN包含3,042个样本，覆盖多样化的分析意图，包括数据过滤、时间分析和可视化编辑，每个样本均配有数据集元信息和设计为反映真实、目标驱动数据分析场景的自然语言查询。我们对14个最先进的开源与闭源LLMs/VLMs进行了基准测试，揭示了在涉及隐含意图、多重交互选项和复杂编辑操作的查询上存在巨大性能差距和频繁失败，凸显交互式界面生成是超越静态图表合成的关键开放挑战。为应对这一挑战，我们提出一种结构化的多阶段界面生成框架，将任务分解为可视化设计表示、生成多个界面候选、约束感知批判与自我优化。该方法使最优模型通过率提升15.9个百分点，展示了通往更可靠语言驱动交互式可视化系统的可行路径。我们已在该网址发布VIS-GEN。
👤 Mizanur Rahman, Aaryaman Kartha, Enamul Hoque Prince 📅 2026/08/11 🔗 arXiv

TSS: Target-Side Sparsification for Speculative Decoding in Domain-Specific Large Language Models
TSS：面向特定领域大语言模型推测解码的目标侧稀疏化
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through collaboration between a lightweight draft model and a target verifier. Existing methods mainly improve the draft side, while the target model is typically kept dense and unchanged. We show that, under domain-specific inference, full-depth target verification is not always the optimal choice. Counter-intuitively, skipping selected target layers can reduce verification cost while simultaneously increasing draft acceptance and preserving, or even improving, downstream task performance. Based on this observation, we propose TSS, a target-side sparsification framework for speculative decoding. TSS employs an acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search to explore multi-layer skip configurations without imposing a fixed priority between the two objectives. The selected configurations are stored in a domain-to-configuration mapping and applied by a lightweight skip controller, allowing one complete target model to support multiple sparse verification paths without retraining or permanent parameter pruning. Experiments on Spec-Bench across multiple domains, model scales, and speculative decoding methods show consistent improvements in draft acceptance and downstream task performance. In Translation setting, TSS increases the average accept length from 2.70 to 4.53 (+67.8%), improves BLEU from 0.131 to 0.237 (+80.9%), and raises end-to-end throughput from 75.6 to 127.3 tokens/s, corresponding to a 1.68X speedup.
中文翻译
推测解码通过轻量级草稿模型与目标验证器之间的协作加速大语言模型推理。现有方法主要优化草稿端，而目标模型通常保持密集且不变。我们发现，在特定领域推理中，全深度目标验证并非总是最优选择。反直觉的是，跳过选定的目标层可以在降低验证成本的同时提高草稿接受率，并保持或甚至提升下游任务性能。基于此观察，我们提出TSS——一种面向推测解码的目标侧稀疏化框架。TSS采用兼顾接受率与指标感知的广度搜索，探索多层跳过配置，不强制在两个目标间设定固定优先级。所选配置存储于领域到配置的映射中，并由轻量级跳过控制器应用，使得单一完整目标模型可支持多个稀疏验证路径，无需重新训练或永久性参数剪枝。在Spec-Bench上的多领域、多模型规模及推测解码方法实验显示，草稿接受率和下游任务性能均有持续提升。在翻译场景中，TSS将平均接受长度从2.70提升至4.53（+67.8%），BLEU从0.131提升至0.237（+80.9%），端到端吞吐量从75.6提升至127.3 tokens/s，对应1.68倍加速。
👤 Haibo Hu, Lianming Huang, Qiao Li, Nan Guan, Chun Jason Xue 📅 2026/08/15 🔗 arXiv

SpecialEduBench: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Knowledge, Skill, and Attitude in Language Intervention for Autistic Children
SpecialEduBench：面向自闭症儿童语言干预中知识、技能与态度的视觉-语言模型基准测试
Language is the target of most early intervention for autistic children. Because the goal and the method change from child to child, the work falls to a teacher who takes one child at a time and judges each scene as it unfolds. Artificial intelligence is now being brought to that work, yet the benchmarks that reach special education ask what a model knows rather than what it does in front of a child. Building one is not straightforward, since whether a response is good teaching depends on what the child has just done, so no answer key applies. The evidence that settles it is visual as much as verbal, since the length of a wait, a shift of gaze, and the child's uptake leave no trace in a transcript. We introduce \emph{SpecialEduBench}, which measures pedagogical competence along knowledge, skill, and attitude, with 4,537 knowledge items and with 200 skill items and 68 attitude items built on recorded intervention, the attitude items crossing pressure with monitoring into 192 response cells. Seven special-education experts wrote, scored, and reviewed the items, and we revised the judge model's instruction against the reference scores they set. Across eight frontier vision-language models no axis is saturated, since the strongest still fails about a tenth of the honesty cells. The models converge where the knowledge is factual and separate where the task is situated, and the failures gather where pressure is applied. We intend the benchmark as an audit to run before deployment and as a starting point for models built for this domain.
中文翻译
语言是针对自闭症儿童早期干预的主要目标。由于每个孩子的目标和方法各不相同，教师需逐个面对孩子并实时判断每一场境况。如今人工智能正被引入这一工作，但现有的特殊教育基准测试关注的是模型‘知道什么’，而非其在真实儿童面前‘做了什么’。构建此类基准并不简单，因为一次回应是否为有效教学取决于孩子刚刚的行为，因此无法提供标准答案。证据既包含语言也包含视觉信息，因为等待时长、视线转移以及孩子的反应在转录文本中并无痕迹。我们提出了 extit{SpecialEduBench}，从知识、技能和态度三个维度衡量教学能力，包含4,537项知识题、200项技能题和68项态度题，所有题目基于真实干预记录构建，其中态度题涵盖压力与监控的192种响应组合。七位特殊教育专家编写、评分并审核了这些题目，我们根据他们设定的参考分数对评判模型的指令进行了修订。在八种前沿视觉-语言模型中，没有一个维度达到饱和，最强模型仍约有十分之一的诚实性单元失败。当任务为事实性知识时模型趋于收敛，而情境化任务则出现分化，失败集中在高压力场景。我们希望该基准能作为部署前的审计工具，并为该领域专用模型的开发提供起点。
👤 Jihoi Na, Taeyeong Kim, Sungjune Kong, Jaemin Jung, Min Joung Park et al. 📅 2026/08/04 🔗 arXiv
[2609.26086]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
CoVeR: Coverage-Based Routing of Verifier Calls in Agentic Retrieval
CoVeR：基于覆盖度的智能体检索中的验证器调用路由机制
An agentic retrieval system issues a sequence of search queries and must decide, at each step, whether the evidence collected so far is enough to stop. Delegating that decision to an LLM verifier or a prompt judge makes stopping reliable, but the verifier then reprocesses the growing evidence after every retrieval step, a substantial repeated cost. We show that most of these calls can be skipped without materially changing answer accuracy: a single threshold on a frozen sentence-embedding coverage margin detects the states in which the evidence is still plainly incomplete, and the verifier is called only on the ambiguous remainder, a gate we call CoVeR (Coverage-based Verifier Routing). Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, with the evaluation protocol fixed before the full-scale run, the CoVeR-gated agent matches the answer accuracy of both the full-budget agent and the always-verify baseline within a fraction of an EM point. It cuts 62-68% of verifier calls, and 93% in a saturated regime. Routers built on evidence counts, lexical overlap, or BM25 relevance, alone or learned in combination, give weaker overall trade-offs, the gate transfers without re-tuning across deciders and agent scales, and its drafter distills into a 921k-parameter head atop the frozen encoder, leaving no LLM in the routing loop. The same signal cannot replace verification: matching a claim is far easier than deciding the claim is supported.
中文翻译
智能体检索系统会发出一系列搜索查询，必须在每一步决定已收集的证据是否足够停止。将此决策委托给LLM验证器或提示判别器可确保停止的可靠性，但验证器需在每次检索后重新处理不断增长的证据，造成显著重复开销。我们发现，大多数验证器调用可以跳过而不影响答案准确率：仅通过一个固定句子嵌入覆盖度阈值即可检测出证据明显不完整的情况，验证器仅在模糊剩余部分被调用，我们称此机制为CoVeR（基于覆盖度的验证器路由）。在三个多跳问答基准上，评估协议在大规模运行前即已固定，CoVeR路由的智能体在答案准确率上与全预算智能体及始终验证基线相比仅相差不足一个EM点。它减少了62%-68%的验证器调用，在饱和状态下甚至高达93%。基于证据数量、词汇重叠或BM25相关性的路由器，无论单独使用还是联合学习，整体权衡效果均较差；该门控机制可在不同决策者和智能体规模间无须重新调参地迁移；其路由头仅需921k参数，且完全不涉及LLM参与路由过程。同一信号无法替代验证：匹配一个主张远比判断该主张是否得到支持容易。
👤 Daeyoung Roh, Donghee Han 📅 2026/09/03 🔗 arXiv

Informed Masking: Structure-Aware Perturbation for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Large Language Models
知情掩码：扩散型大语言模型强化学习中的结构感知扰动
Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as an efficient alternative to autoregressive models, yet aligning them via Reinforcement Learning (RL) requires likelihood surrogates estimated from masked reconstruction subproblems under a small Monte Carlo budget per rollout. Existing methods construct these subproblems by uniform random masking, leaving open the question of which subproblems to prioritize. We identify a systematic upstream/downstream structure in dLLM rollouts. Some tokens, when revealed, trigger large confidence changes in nearby undecoded positions; we call them upstream. Others induce only small local changes and are therefore downstream. We find masking downstream tokens yields substantially better-posed subproblems than masking upstream tokens, a phenomenon we term subproblem difficulty asymmetry. Based on the observation, we propose Informed Masking (IM), which derives a per-token priority score from the denoising trajectory at zero extra inference cost and biases mask sampling toward downstream tokens. IM is plug-and-play: when plugged into three state-of-the-art dLLM RL methods on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, it delivers up to 2.01%, 8.68%, and 5.77% relative average gains on math and planning benchmarks with improved training stability.
中文翻译
扩散型大语言模型（dLLMs）已成为自回归模型的有效替代方案，但通过强化学习（RL）对齐它们需要在每次回溯的少量蒙特卡洛预算下估计似然代理。现有方法通过均匀随机掩码构建这些子问题，未解决应优先处理哪些子问题的问题。我们识别出dLLM回溯中的系统性上下游结构：某些标记在揭示后会引发附近未解码位置的显著置信度变化，我们称之为上游；而另一些仅引起微小局部变化，因此为下游。我们发现掩码下游标记比掩码上游标记能产生更优的子问题，这一现象称为子问题难度不对称。基于此观察，我们提出知情掩码（IM），从去噪轨迹中以零额外推理成本推导每个标记的优先级分数，并使掩码采样偏向下游标记。IM可即插即用：在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上的三种先进dLLM RL方法中应用时，其在数学和规划基准上分别带来最高2.01%、8.68%和5.77%的相对平均提升，并改善了训练稳定性。
👤 Xiaoyi Yu, Enver Sangineto, Pei Fu, Fiorenzo Parascandolo, Wenhui Tan et al. 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Qwen3.8-Omni: Towards Native Omni-Modal Agents
Qwen3.8-Omni：迈向原生多模态智能体
We introduce Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, a natively multimodal agentic model for real-world multimodal productivity. Compared with previous omni models, which primarily emphasized perception and interaction, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash substantially improves multimodal understanding and reasoning, as well as performance on long-horizon agentic tasks. These capabilities are supported by a native multimodal co-training strategy that preserves strong text-domain capabilities while facilitating the transfer of agentic capabilities from text to audio and video tasks. The model inherits the sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture of Qwen3.8-Next and extends the context window to one million tokens, supporting long-context multimodal reasoning and long-horizon planning. These advances enable integration into production workflows as a primary agent or a specialized sub-agent, supporting video editing, long-form audio and video translation, music-conditioned music video or movie generation, and video-based note or omni-skill creation. To address the lack of native audio and video support in existing agent harnesses, we release Qwen-MM-Plugins, a lightweight open-source plugin framework for multimodal productivity. We further frame real-time multimodal interaction as a system-level challenge requiring orchestration of context and memory management, tool use, and sub-agent delegation. Accordingly, we release Qwen-Live-Harness, an open-source framework for building responsive, real-time multimodal agents based on Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash achieves strong performance across multimodal understanding, reasoning, long-horizon agentic execution, and video productivity tasks. These results and the accompanying open-source tools support Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash as a practical foundation for deploying natively multimodal agents in research and production.
中文翻译
我们介绍了Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash，一种用于真实世界多模态生产力的原生多模态智能体模型。与以往以感知和交互为重点的全模态模型不同，Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash显著提升了多模态理解与推理能力，以及在长周期智能体任务中的表现。这一能力得益于一种原生多模态联合训练策略，在保持强大文本领域能力的同时，促进了从文本到音频和视频任务的智能体能力迁移。该模型继承了Qwen3.8-Next的稀疏专家混合（MoE）架构，并将上下文窗口扩展至一百万token，支持长上下文多模态推理与长周期规划。这些进展使其可作为主智能体或专用子智能体集成到生产工作流中，支持视频编辑、长篇音频与视频翻译、音乐条件生成音乐视频或电影、基于视频的笔记或全技能创建等任务。为解决现有智能体框架缺乏原生音视频支持的问题，我们发布了Qwen-MM-Plugins，一个轻量级开源多模态生产力插件框架。我们进一步将实时多模态交互视为一个系统级挑战，需要协调上下文与记忆管理、工具使用及子智能体委派。为此，我们发布了Qwen-Live-Harness，一个基于Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash构建响应式、实时多模态智能体的开源框架。大量评估表明，Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash在多模态理解、推理、长周期智能体执行和视频生产力任务中均表现出色。这些结果及配套开源工具支持Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash成为研究与生产中部署原生多模态智能体的实用基础。

Conduct Under Pressure: What Sixty Language Models Do When a User Pushes
压力下的表现：六十余个语言模型在用户施压时的行为分析
We study what LLMs do when a user applies pressure in an uncomfortable situation: a user insists, begs, flatters or grieves, and the model gives up a correct fact, writes a document it should refuse, or cheers a plan that will cost the user money. We send frozen multi-turn scenes, identical for every model regardless of the reply, to 60 models from 13 vendors, and label each transcript with a codebook built by open coding and then frozen: a trajectory (the model held its position or folded) and a manner (how it held or folded). Two findings separate. Whether a model holds tracks its generation, meaning how recent it is: fold rate correlates with a public capability index at Spearman -0.64, with little vendor effect. How it holds tracks the vendor: six of the 17 manner codes sort by vendor at permutation p <= 0.001, corrected across the codebook. We report four vendor profiles on the codes that cleared reliability. We also ask which parts of the labeling need a person. Six LLM coders from three vendors apply the codebook more consistently than three human coders do (Krippendorff's alpha 0.66 against 0.46), agree with the codebook's author on trajectory at kappa 0.84 to 0.91 on transcripts the codebook's examples never touched, and match an adjudicated human reference at 0.83. Blind machine readings recover the codebook's categories but cannot tell which of them a second reader would apply the same way. We conclude that for behavior a non-specialist can judge, the human contribution is authoring and bounding the codes and owning a small reference, not producing labels at volume.
中文翻译
我们研究了当用户在不适情境下施加压力时，大语言模型（LLM）的反应：用户坚持、恳求、奉承或哀求，模型放弃正确事实、撰写本应拒绝的文档，或支持一项将使用户付出代价的计划。我们将相同多轮场景发送给来自13家厂商的60个模型，无论回复如何，场景保持一致，并使用由开放式编码构建后冻结的代码本对每段转录进行标注：轨迹（模型坚持立场或屈服）和方式（如何坚持或屈服）。两个关键发现分离：模型是否屈服与其生成时间相关，即越新的模型屈服率越高，屈服率与公开能力指数呈Spearman相关系数-0.64，厂商影响较小。而其坚持方式则与厂商相关：17种方式代码中有6种在厂商间排序具有统计显著性（置换p ≤ 0.001，经代码本校正）。我们报告了四个通过可靠性检验的厂商画像。此外，我们探讨了标注中哪些部分需人工参与。来自三家厂商的六名LLM标注员比三名人类标注员更一致（Krippendorff's alpha 0.66 vs 0.46），在代码本示例未覆盖的转录上与代码本作者在轨迹判断上达到kappa 0.84至0.91的一致性，且与仲裁人类参考标准匹配度达0.83。盲测机器读取能恢复代码本类别，但无法判断第二位阅读者是否会以相同方式应用。我们得出结论：对于非专业人士可判断的行为，人类的贡献在于制定和界定代码，拥有少量参考样本，而非大规模生产标签。

Passes Alone, Fails Together: Benchmarking Semantic Coordination in Parallel LLM-Agent Development
单独通过，合并失败：并行大语言模型智能体开发中的语义协调基准测试
Parallel coding agents can produce patches that work alone but fail when merged. This happens when one agent changes an interface or rule that another agent still relies on. We study these failures with stale, a benchmark for semantic coordination. Our evaluation runs the same tests on each patch alone and on their combination, counting only failures introduced by combining the patches. We use three tiers: synthetic tasks with controlled interface changes, pairs of merged pull requests, and constructed tasks that use real Django helpers. Among 834 runs on 417 mined Django pairs, only one showed interference after correcting the grading procedure. On constructed tasks using 12 Django helpers, interference occurred in 97% of runs. A message describing the completed concurrent change recovered 82% of runs. Reviewed pull requests may contain few unresolved parallel changes, even when agents fail on controlled tasks using real code. The constructed failure rates do not estimate how often these problems occur in practice.
中文翻译
并行编码智能体生成的补丁可能单独运行正常，但在合并后失败。这是由于一个智能体更改了另一个智能体仍依赖的接口或规则。我们通过stale这一语义协调基准测试研究此类失败。评估在每个补丁单独运行和组合运行时执行相同测试，仅统计由合并引入的失败。评估分为三个层级：具有受控接口变化的合成任务、已合并的拉取请求对，以及使用真实Django工具构建的任务。在417个挖掘出的Django补丁对中进行834次运行，修正评分流程后仅一次出现干扰。在使用12个Django工具构建的任务中，97%的运行出现干扰。描述已完成并发变更的消息可恢复82%的运行。即使在真实代码的受控任务中智能体失败，审查中的拉取请求也可能包含很少未解决的并行变更。构建的失败率并不能准确估计这些问题在实际中的发生频率。

FinFIRST: Benchmarking Search Agents for Financial Information Retrieval, Sourcing and Traceability
FinFIRST：金融信息检索、溯源与可追溯性的搜索代理基准测试
Financial search is a highly demanding task for LLM agents, requiring not only a correct final answer but also temporally valid information retrieval, authoritative source selection, entity and period alignment, unit and definition consistency, and verifiable evidence for all conclusions. Existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate only the final answer, making it difficult to localize errors or assess whether an answer is well-founded. To address this gap, we introduce FinFIRST (Financial Information Retrieval, Sourcing and Traceability), the first financial benchmark to jointly evaluate answers and supporting evidence through atomic rubrics. FinFIRST comprises 123 expert-authored tasks spanning a graduated difficulty spectrum, constructed from aggregate patterns of real-world financial scenarios through an 18-field taxonomy, a six-axis coverage blueprint, a registry of 138 financial sources, contributions from over 50 finance experts, and a six-stage quality-control pipeline. Each task is accompanied by an evidence-grounded reference package decomposed into atomic criteria across three dimensions: raw-information acquisition, source verification, and computation and answer formation. We evaluate 15 model configurations under a unified tool setting. Claude-Opus-5 achieves the highest atomic score of 87.59%, while GPT-5.6-Sol attains the highest strict pass rate of 71.54%. Computation and answer formation consistently lag behind raw-information acquisition across systems. FinFIRST retains final-answer correctness as the primary objective while making the supporting research process measurable, verifiable, and diagnosable.
中文翻译
金融搜索是LLM智能体的一项高要求任务，不仅需要正确的最终答案，还需时间有效的信息检索、权威来源选择、实体与时期对齐、单位与定义一致性，以及所有结论的可验证证据。现有基准主要只评估最终答案，使得错误定位困难，也难以判断答案是否基于充分依据。为弥补这一差距，我们引入FinFIRST（金融信息检索、溯源与可追溯性），首个联合评估答案与支持证据的金融基准，采用原子评分标准。FinFIRST包含123个专家撰写的任务，涵盖从现实金融场景中归纳出的渐进难度谱，基于18个领域分类、六轴覆盖蓝图、138个金融来源名录、超过50位金融专家贡献，以及六阶段质量控制流程构建。每个任务均配有基于证据的参考包，分解为三个维度的原子标准：原始信息获取、来源验证、计算与答案形成。我们在统一工具设置下评估15种模型配置。Claude-Opus-5获得最高的原子得分为87.59%，而GPT-5.6-Sol达到最高的严格通过率为71.54%。各系统在计算与答案形成方面始终落后于原始信息获取。FinFIRST在保持最终答案正确性为主要目标的同时，使支持的研究过程变得可测量、可验证且可诊断。

[2609.25130]

Impact Is Not Invalidation: Ask About the Claim, Not the Diff
影响不等于无效：应询问主张本身，而非差异内容
Memory systems for coding agents must decide, when a repository changes, which of their stored claims have become false. Content anchoring invalidates a claim whenever the artifact it came from changes, which fires constantly. Semantic-equivalence classification asks whether a diff preserves behavior, a question about the diff rather than about any stored claim. We show the second signal fails for a reason unrelated to model capability: asked whether a commit preserves behavior, five models spanning a 40x price range fire on 59-72% of real commits and reach precisions of only 0.291 to 0.329 against a 0.25 base rate. Asked instead whether one specific claim still holds, the same models on the same diffs reach 0.705 to 0.974. A control that hands the behavior-preservation judge the claim text, changing only the question, moves precision by 0.010 and 0.016; changing the question moves it by 0.49 and 0.65. We also compare against pytest-testmon, a deployed regression-test selector with coverage-derived dependency data: it reaches 0.868 recall at 0.415 precision, so near-complete knowledge of what a change can reach does not identify what it falsifies. Ground truth is execution, not annotation: a claim is a test function passing at commit t, and it has flipped if that same assertion text fails at t+1. Building this required an observation we did not find in prior work. On a CI-gated mainline a commit that leaves a pre-existing test failing cannot merge, so the naive construction has an empty positive class by design. We report 10,369 claims with 184 execution-verified flips mined from 23 Python libraries, splits held out by repository, a post-knowledge-cutoff split, a shuffled-diff null, a paraphrase control, and a leave-one-repository-out analysis over 17 repositories.
中文翻译
编码智能体的记忆系统必须决定，在仓库发生变化时，其存储的哪些主张已变为虚假。内容锚定在所来自的工件发生变化时即失效，这会频繁触发。语义等价分类则询问差异是否保留行为，这是一个关于差异的问题，而非关于任何存储主张的问题。我们证明第二个信号失效的原因与模型能力无关：当询问一个提交是否保留行为时，五个跨越40倍价格范围的模型在真实提交中触发率达59%-72%，精度仅为0.291至0.329，远低于0.25的基线。而当改为询问某个具体主张是否仍然成立时，相同模型在相同差异上精度提升至0.705至0.974。一个对照实验将行为保留判断者提供主张文本，仅改变问题，精度提升0.010和0.016；而仅改变问题，精度提升0.49和0.65。我们还与pytest-testmon（一个部署的回归测试选择器，具有基于覆盖率的依赖数据）进行对比：其召回率为0.868，精度为0.415，说明对变更可达范围的近乎完整知识，并不能识别其导致的虚假主张。真实情况是执行结果，而非注释：一个主张是一个在提交t时通过的测试函数，若同一断言文本在t+1时失败，则该主张已翻转。构建此数据需要我们发现的一个此前文献中未提及的观察：在CI门控主干线上，若一个提交使已有测试失败，则无法合并，因此朴素构造的正类为空。我们报告了从23个Python库中挖掘出的10,369个主张，其中184个经过执行验证的翻转，数据按仓库划分、知识截止后划分、随机差异空模型、改写对照和17个仓库的留一仓库分析。
👤 Atul Anand 📅 2026/09/20 🔗 arXiv
[2609.25066]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Understanding Reliability in LLM-based Human Behavior Simulation
理解基于大语言模型的人类行为模拟的可靠性
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human survey responses and behavioral reactions, yet unreliable simulations can mislead social science conclusions. However, existing evaluations focus on end-to-end scores, leaving it unclear how different aspects of the simulation process interact to determine reliability. We propose ReliMap, which decomposes LLM-based human behavior simulation into three structured layers and evaluates reliability at both the individual level (R1) and population level (R2) across three configuration dimensions: model capacity, profile completeness, and population coverage. Through experiments across four simulation tasks and eleven LLMs, we find that all models exhibit substantial distributional bias without profile conditioning. Profile conditioning reduces this bias with diminishing returns. Larger models benefit more, and attribute informativeness matters more than quantity. Critically, R1 gains do not reliably transfer to R2--individual and population-level reliability can move in opposite directions. At the population layer, increasing coverage reduces variance but not systematic bias, with R2 stabilizing at around 50-100 individuals. These findings highlight that reliable simulation cannot be achieved by optimizing any single layer in isolation, but requires coordinated improvement across all three.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于模拟人类调查回答和行为反应，但不可靠的模拟可能误导社会科学结论。然而，现有评估主要关注端到端得分，未能揭示模拟过程中不同环节如何相互作用以决定可靠性。我们提出ReliMap，将基于LLM的人类行为模拟分解为三个结构化层级，并在个体层面（R1）和群体层面（R2）评估可靠性，涵盖模型容量、个人资料完整性与群体覆盖度三个配置维度。通过对四个模拟任务和十一种LLM的实验，我们发现所有模型在缺乏个人资料条件时均表现出显著分布偏差；个人资料条件虽能降低偏差，但收益递减；更大的模型受益更多，且属性信息的重要性高于数量。关键的是，R1的提升并不能可靠转移到R2——个体与群体层面的可靠性可能朝相反方向变化。在群体层级，增加覆盖范围可减少方差但无法消除系统性偏差，R2在约50-100人时趋于稳定。这些发现表明，可靠的模拟不能通过孤立优化任一层次实现，而需在三个层级间协同改进。
👤 Pei Wang, Lei Wang, Yuanzi Li, Xu Chen 📅 2026/09/16 🔗 arXiv
[2609.25056]

Graph-Based Inference for Feedback-Driven Word Deduction: A Scalable Framework for the Jotto Problem
基于图推理的反馈驱动词汇推断：Jotto问题的可扩展框架
A feedback-based word deduction framework based on the Jotto problem is proposed, and the problem space is represented as a weighted graph where all valid words correspond to nodes, and the edge weight is defined by the number of common letters between the two words. Finally, the gameplay is defined as an iterative constraint propagation mechanism where feedback is used to iteratively narrow the incompatible space of the graph, facilitating the reduction of the hypothesis space in a structured and interpretable manner. In contrast to existing approaches, where the problem space is typically defined for fixed-length isograms, the proposed framework generalizes to variable-length words (between 3 and 8 letters) and naturally extends to repeated letter cases, facilitating the treatment of realistic Jotto problem instances within a unified framework for the first time. The proposed framework's applicability and solver dynamics are also discussed through an interactive implementation and a qualitative case study, respectively. Significant automated tests on approximately 3,000 simulated gameplay scenarios identify a novel convergence behavior: the expected number of iterations diminishes with increasing word length. A strong relationship is confirmed using statistical tests to verify a logarithmic relationship, which is also verified using regression modeling and goodness-of-fit tests. In addition to the initial problem statement, this formulation introduces graph pruning as a viable paradigm for feedback-driven inference with interpretability and its association with symbolic reasoning and interactive intelligent systems.
中文翻译
本文提出一种基于反馈的词汇推断框架，以Jotto问题为基础，将问题空间建模为加权图，其中所有有效词汇对应节点，边权重定义为两词间的共有字母数。游戏过程被定义为一种迭代约束传播机制，利用反馈逐步缩小图中不兼容的区域，从而以结构化且可解释的方式缩减假设空间。与现有方法通常限定于固定长度等字符词不同，该框架可推广至变长词汇（3至8个字母）并自然处理重复字母情况，首次在统一框架下处理现实中的Jotto问题实例。通过交互式实现和定性案例研究，探讨了该框架的适用性与求解动态。对约3000个模拟游戏场景的自动化测试揭示了一种新收敛行为：预期迭代次数随词长增加而减少。统计检验确认了这种关系呈对数形式，并通过回归建模与拟合优度检验得到验证。除初始问题设定外，该形式还引入图剪枝作为反馈驱动推理的可行范式，具有可解释性，并与符号推理及交互式智能系统相关联。

ICDAR2026 Competition on Multimodal Reasoning over Documents in Multiple Domains
ICDAR2026多领域文档多模态推理竞赛报告
In this report we present results of the ICDAR2026 Competition on Multimodal Reasoning over Documents in Multiple Domains. This competition aimed to advance research in document understanding through the task of Visual Question Answering (VQA). Building upon previous DocVQA benchmarks, this competition introduces challenging reasoning questions over a diverse collection of documents spanning eight domains, including business reports, scientific papers, slides, posters, maps, comics, infographics, and engineering drawings. The competition concluded with 20 valid submissions from 8 teams spanning zero-shot VLMs, OCR and parser-augmented pipelines, agentic retrieval systems, multi-agent ensembles, and fine-tuned multimodal models. The results show that the strongest systems move beyond single-pass prompting and instead rely on structured evidence extraction, retrieval, verification, and orchestration across multiple components.

agent-benchmark
FrontierMath Erdős
FrontierMath Erdős
We introduce FrontierMath Erdős (FME), a benchmark of 68 Erdős problems that are open as of August 2026. To solve a task in FME, AI systems must resolve (prove or disprove) one of the 68 conjectures in the proof assistant Lean. Our 68 problems were selected by the second author among 652 open problems on this http URL for their mathematical interest and difficulty. AIs have recently resolved several open problems in mathematics, but these demonstrations fall short of a systematic study of AI capabilities. FME evaluates every AI model on the same fixed problems, autonomously and under the same budget. We evaluated five AIs with a budget of \$300 per problem. One (GPT-6 Astra) scored 3%, and all others scored 0%.

Prompt Breadth and Rollout Refresh Interact in On-Policy Distillation
提示广度与滚动刷新在策略内蒸馏中的交互作用
How many prompts does on-policy distillation (OPD) need, and how does the answer depend on the student policies that generate its training responses? We study these two controls jointly: prompt breadth and rollout refresh. A 3x3 mathematical-reasoning experiment fixes 14,080 trajectories and 110 optimizer updates while varying the prompt bank and the number of response-generating policy snapshots. With ten snapshots, eight prompts reach 24.09% average accuracy, close to 24.51% for 14,080 distinct prompts. With responses frozen at the initial policy, however, increasing breadth lowers accuracy from 21.16% to 19.05%; under per-update refresh, it raises accuracy from 23.61% to 25.57%. The resulting interaction is 4.07 percentage points, with a 95% question-paired interval of [2.00, 6.28]. Matched comparisons under two teachers reveal a second reversal: the periodic models have higher short-budget accuracy and answer completion, but frozen-response models overtake in average accuracy at a 32K output limit, using 1.7-1.8x as many response tokens. These results show that prompt efficiency in OPD can depend on both refresh and inference budget.
中文翻译
策略内蒸馏（OPD）需要多少提示？这个问题的答案如何依赖于生成训练响应的学生策略？我们联合研究了两个控制变量：提示广度和滚动刷新。一项3x3的数学推理实验固定了14,080条轨迹和110次优化器更新，同时改变提示库和生成响应的策略快照数量。当使用十次快照时，八条提示达到24.09%的平均准确率，接近于14,080条不同提示的24.51%。然而，在初始策略响应冻结的情况下，增加提示广度会使准确率从21.16%下降至19.05%；而在每次更新后刷新的情况下，准确率则从23.61%上升至25.57%。由此产生的交互效应为4.07个百分点，95%配对置信区间为[2.00, 6.28]。在两种教师模型下的匹配比较中还发现第二个反转现象：周期性模型在短预算下具有更高的准确率和回答完成度，但在32K输出限制下，冻结响应模型在平均准确率上超越前者，且使用的响应token数量仅为1.7-1.8倍。这些结果表明，OPD中的提示效率取决于刷新策略和推理预算。
👤 Lingxiang Hu, Tianle Xia, Ming Xu, Yiding Sun, Linfang Shang 📅 2026/09/06 🔗 arXiv

Peerify: Benchmarking Peer-Review Claim Verification
Peerify：同行评审主张验证的基准测试
Peer review plays a central role in scholarly publishing, yet verifying whether reviewer claims are supported by manuscript evidence remains a largely manual and time-consuming process. We present Peerify, a pipeline for manuscript-grounded verification of peer-review claims. Given a manuscript and a review comment, the Peerify pipeline decomposes reviews into atomic claims, retrieves relevant manuscript evidence, and determines whether each claim is supported by the paper. To support the development and evaluation of the pipeline, we construct a benchmark of 800 claims derived from authentic peer-review interactions collected from NeurIPS 2024 and ICLR 2024, including a 300-claim hand-labeled subset used to audit the automated supervision. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models and retrieval strategies within the Peerify pipeline, together with entailment baselines. Our results demonstrate the importance of retrieval-centered verification and claim decomposition, while highlighting the challenges posed by ambiguous and interpretive reviewer claims. Automated labels agree with human consensus on 90.3% of audited claims ($\kappa = 0.87$), while off-the-shelf entailment models stay below 0.24 macro-F1.
中文翻译
同行评审在学术出版中起着核心作用，但验证审稿人主张是否得到手稿证据支持，仍主要依赖人工且耗时。我们提出Peerify，一个基于手稿证据的同行评审主张验证流水线。给定一篇手稿和一条审稿意见，Peerify流水线将评论分解为原子主张，检索相关手稿证据，并判断每项主张是否被论文支持。为支持该流水线的开发与评估，我们构建了一个包含800个主张的基准数据集，这些主张源自NeurIPS 2024和ICLR 2024的真实同行评审互动，其中包含一个300个主张的手动标注子集，用于审计自动化监督。我们在Peerify流水线中评估了最先进的语言模型和检索策略，以及蕴含基线模型。结果表明，以检索为中心的验证和主张分解至关重要，同时揭示了模糊和解释性审稿主张带来的挑战。自动化标签与人类共识在90.3%的审计主张上一致（κ = 0.87），而现成的蕴含模型宏F1值均低于0.24。

Behavior is Not Enough: A Mechanism-Based Evaluation of Social Norm Emergence in LLM Societies
行为不足以说明问题：基于机制的社会规范涌现评估
Social norms cannot be identified from behavior alone: the same cooperative equilibrium may reflect shared expectations, strategic incentives, or simple imitation. Yet in multi-agent large language model systems, prior work largely treats behavioral convergence as evidence of norm emergence. In this work, we introduce an evaluation framework that measures agents' reported empirical and normative expectations in addition to behavioral convergence. Through controlled ablations, we test the effect of expectation elicitation and isolate two collective mechanisms central to theories of norm formation---social learning through interaction and social selection through network-based group formation. We further test the stability of these resulting dynamics under adversarial disruption across four LLM families. We find that eliciting expectations increases cooperative contributions, while social learning stabilizes behavior, and social selection reliably identifies cooperators but provides limited behavioral reinforcement. Following disruption, normative expectations and behavioral coordination recover differently. Together, these results show that similar cooperative outcomes can arise from different underlying social processes. By making expectations observable, our framework allows us to attribute each mechanism's contribution separately, offering designers of multi-agent systems a principled basis for selecting the social processes that sustain cooperation.
中文翻译
仅从行为无法识别社会规范：相同的合作均衡可能反映共同预期、策略激励或简单模仿。然而，在多智能体大语言模型系统中，先前工作大多将行为收敛视为规范涌现的证据。本文引入一种评估框架，不仅测量代理的行为收敛，还评估其报告的经验性与规范性预期。通过受控消融实验，我们测试了预期提取的影响，并分离出规范形成理论中的两个核心集体机制——通过交互的社会学习和基于网络的群体形成带来的社会选择。我们进一步在四个大语言模型家族中测试了这些动态在对抗性干扰下的稳定性。结果发现，提取预期能提高合作贡献，社会学习能稳定行为，社会选择能可靠识别合作者但对行为强化有限。干扰后，规范性预期与行为协调的恢复方式不同。这些结果表明，相似的合作成果可能源于不同的社会过程。通过使预期可见，我们的框架允许分别归因每个机制的贡献，为多智能体系统设计者提供了选择维持合作的社会过程的合理依据。
👤 Rasika Muralidharan, Haewoon Kwak, Jisun An 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

ABAI at COLIEE 2026 Task 1: Multi-Stage Retrieval with GraphRAG-Enhanced Meta-Learning, and a Post-Hoc Study of the Cross-Validation-to-Test Gap
ABAI在COLIEE 2026任务1中的表现：基于GraphRAG增强元学习的多阶段检索及交叉验证到测试集差距的后验研究
We present the ABAI submission to COLIEE 2026 Task 1, case law retrieval, together with a controlled study of why it underperformed. The task suppresses the cited passages themselves, which removes much of the lexical overlap a retriever would rely on. Our pipeline answers this with four independently trained stages: multi-view BM25 over citation-context windows with reciprocal rank fusion, neural reranking, graph-based features from entity communities and a graph attention network, and a LightGBM meta-learner over 34 features. Our best run reached F1=0.177 on the official test set, against a cross-validated 0.311, and we attributed that gap to a recall ceiling, temporal distribution shift, and threshold miscalibration. We then tested all three. Under leakage-free protocols threshold transfer costs 0.007 F1, decision quality is flat across chronological quartiles, and the official test queries are not measurably farther from the training manifold than training queries are from each other, in two independent embedding spaces. Decomposing the misses instead splits them exactly evenly between candidates never retrieved and candidates retrieved but ranked below the cut. Measuring the remedies for each half, BM25 length-normalisation tuning, an event-triple view, and full-content dense fusion lift top-200 recall by three to seven points, and citation-graph features add 0.014 F1 over eight seeds once own-citation leakage is removed, while per-query cutoff rules, a zero-shot reranker swap, and a date filter do not help. We also document four evaluation artifacts, each of which reversed a result once the protocol was corrected.
中文翻译
我们展示了ABAI对COLIEE 2026任务1（判例法检索）的提交结果，并进行了受控研究以解释其表现不佳的原因。该任务屏蔽了引用段落本身，从而大幅减少了检索器依赖的词汇重叠。我们的流水线采用四个独立训练的阶段：基于引用上下文窗口的多视角BM25与倒数排名融合、神经重排序、基于实体社区的图特征与图注意力网络，以及基于34个特征的LightGBM元学习器。最佳运行在官方测试集上达到F1=0.177，而交叉验证得分高达0.311。我们将其差距归因于召回上限、时间分布偏移和阈值校准不当。随后我们测试了这三个因素。在无泄漏协议下，阈值迁移导致F1损失0.007，决策质量在时间四分位区间内保持平稳，且官方测试查询与训练流形的距离并未显著超出训练查询之间的距离（在两个独立嵌入空间中）。分解失败案例发现，未检索到的候选者与被检索但排名低于截断点的候选者各占一半。针对每部分进行修复措施评估：BM25长度归一化调优、事件三元组视图、全内容密集融合可使top-200召回率提升3至7个百分点；去除自引泄露后，引用图特征在八次种子实验中额外带来0.014 F1提升；而每查询截断规则、零样本重排序替换和日期过滤均无效。我们还记录了四个评估伪影，每个在协议修正后都曾反转一次结果。

WatchPoint: Executable User Feedback for Real-World Agentic Web Development
WatchPoint：面向真实世界智能体Web开发的可执行用户反馈
When a professional web developer's code fails a test, they do not simply re-read the stack trace. They open the application in a browser, click buttons, inspect computed styles, and run diagnostic commands to understand what went wrong. Existing feedback mechanisms for coding agents rely on screenshots, LLM-as-a-judge scoring, or natural-language corrections, but few interact with the live application the way a developer would. We introduce WatchPoint, a simulated-user system that mimics real developer behavior by generating and executing diagnostic scripts against the running application, producing structured observations that guide the coding model's retry. Unlike prior approaches that target single-file edits or evaluate using non-executable metrics, we operate on Web-Bench, a benchmark of 50 multi-file web projects comprising 1,000 sequentially dependent tasks, verified by deterministic end-to-end tests. WatchPoint recovers 57.6% of the tasks it diagnoses, and a controlled user study confirms the simulation's realism: human testers achieve a comparable recovery rate (54.5%), providing evidence that automated diagnostic scripts can substitute for interactive human testing on sequential web development tasks. We further identify a pattern of capability gaps that governs when simulated-user feedback is helpful and when it should be withheld.
中文翻译
当专业Web开发者编写的代码未能通过测试时，他们不会仅仅重新阅读堆栈跟踪。他们会打开浏览器中的应用程序，点击按钮，检查计算样式，并运行诊断命令以理解问题所在。现有的编码智能体反馈机制依赖于截图、LLM作为裁判评分或自然语言修正，但很少像开发者那样与实时应用进行交互。我们提出了WatchPoint，一种模拟用户系统，通过生成并执行针对运行中应用的诊断脚本，模仿真实开发者的操作行为，产生结构化观察结果来指导编码模型的重试。与以往仅针对单文件修改或使用非可执行指标评估的方法不同，我们的方法基于Web-Bench基准，该基准包含50个多文件Web项目，共1000个顺序依赖任务，并通过确定性端到端测试验证。WatchPoint成功恢复了其诊断的57.6%的任务，受控用户研究也证实了模拟的真实性：人类测试者达到了相近的恢复率（54.5%），表明自动化诊断脚本可以在顺序Web开发任务中替代交互式人工测试。我们还识别出一种能力差距模式，决定了何时模拟用户反馈有用，何时应予以保留。
👤 Guanqun Yang, Wei Yang, Xueqing Liu 📅 2026/08/10 🔗 arXiv

FIRE: Failure-Informed Runtime Engineering for Reliable Language-Model Agents
FIRE：面向可靠语言模型智能体的故障感知运行时工程
Language-model agents often reach a working solution and then fail to consistently deliver it. We study runtime policies: targeted natural-language instructions and action denials applied by the agent harness at states that preceded observed failures, without changing model weights or the user prompt. With this, keeping capability constant, we observe a meaningful unlock in delivered reliability. Across the complete 87-task Terminal-Bench 2.1 suite, with two attempts per task, policies increase repeated success (pass^2) in all three GPT-5.6 tiers: 50.6% to 54.0% for Luna, 55.2% to 60.9% for Terra, and 64.4% to 73.6% for Sol. Sol's best-of-two success changes by 1.2 points while repeated success rises by 9.2, showing that policies chiefly convert reachable solutions into dependable delivery. We further cover 14 tasks under Terra's frozen portfolio. Policy-guided Terra reaches 71.4%, compared with 64.3% for unassisted Sol, at about half the cost, demonstrating how engineering around models could unlock dependability for a use case. To isolate the mechanism we run a randomized five-arm experiment: real policies reach 61% on eligible tasks, versus 39% without a policy, 36% with a timing-matched sham, and 39 to 43% with generic verification or reconsideration. The intended corrective behavior appears in 22 of 24 coded policy attempts, against at most 14 in any other arm. Runtime policies are therefore a practical reliability layer: they make capabilities an agent already possesses substantially more repeatable.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体常常能够找到可行的解决方案，但却无法持续交付。我们研究了运行时策略：在预观测失败状态时，由智能体施加的目标性自然语言指令和动作拒绝，而不改变模型权重或用户提示。在此前提下，保持能力不变，我们观察到交付可靠性显著提升。在完整的87项Terminal-Bench 2.1套件中，每项任务两次尝试，策略在所有三个GPT-5.6层级中均提升了重复成功率（pass^2）：Luna从50.6%升至54.0%，Terra从55.2%升至60.9%，Sol从64.4%升至73.6%。Sol的最佳两次成功率仅提高1.2个百分点，而重复成功率上升了9.2个百分点，表明策略主要将可达解转化为可靠交付。我们进一步覆盖了Terra冻结组合中的14项任务。策略引导的Terra达到71.4%，相比未辅助的Sol（64.3%）提升显著，且成本仅为一半，展示了围绕模型进行工程优化如何为特定用例解锁可靠性。为隔离机制，我们进行了随机五臂实验：真实策略在合格任务上达到61%，而无策略组为39%，时间匹配假策略为36%，通用验证或重新考虑策略为39%至43%。预期纠正行为出现在24次编码策略尝试中的22次，而其他任何一组最多仅14次。因此，运行时策略是一种实用的可靠性层：它们使智能体已具备的能力变得更具重复性。
👤 Nikita Agarwal, Nivedit Jain 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Certified Against Which Oracle? Execution Labels Set the Reported Risk of Conformal Abstention for Text-to-SQL
认证针对的是哪个预言机？执行标签决定了文本到SQL的合规范式拒答报告风险
A conformal abstention certificate for text-to-SQL is only as truthful as the correctness labels it is calibrated on. The uncertainty pipelines that read confidence off execution consistency take those labels from the single database a benchmark ships, an oracle known to be lenient. We run a preregistered intervention on Spider-Realistic, swapping that database for the benchmark's distilled multi-instance test suite. Across four SQL-specialist checkpoints and two split schemes, the swap raises the certificate's held-out risk 2.73 to 10.23 points above the risk its own labels report. Neither oracle reports the risk experts assign. Under blinded labels from two SQL experts, a certificate calibrated at a nominal 0.10 carries 20.0 and 17.2 points of risk on two checkpoints. The stricter oracle errs in both directions: most of the answers it rejects are not judged wrong, and some of those it accepts are. An AI-assigned census of what it rejects finds a semantic error in a quarter to a third of them, depending on the population. It attributes most of the rest to underspecified questions, synthetic instances or suspected reference-query defects, a flag supported by a preregistered blinded expert audit. The oracle also decides how a confidence score is judged. Every execution-consistency score looks better under the labels of the oracle that built its clusters, in 16 of 16 combinations. Under expert labels, building such a score on suite clusters instead of shipped-database clusters raises its area under the ROC curve (AUROC) by 6.96 points on one checkpoint and 1.53 on the other. On the second, the expert interval excludes the 8.3 points the suite labels report. A certificate should be reported with both oracles, and an oracle-relative difference read as semantic risk only after the benchmark is audited. A consistency score should be evaluated under an oracle that did not build it.
中文翻译
文本到SQL的合规范式拒答证书的真实性取决于其校准所依据的正确性标签。依赖执行一致性获取置信度的不确定性流程，从基准自带的单一数据库中获取这些标签，而该数据库已知较为宽松。我们在Spider-Realistic上进行了一次预注册干预，将该数据库替换为基准的蒸馏多实例测试套件。在四个SQL专家检查点和两种划分方案下，这一替换使证书的保留风险相比其自身标签报告的风险上升了2.73至10.23个百分点。两个预言机均未反映专家所评定的风险。在两名SQL专家提供的盲标下，一个名义置信度为0.10的证书在两个检查点上分别表现出20.0和17.2的风险点。更严格的预言机在两个方向上均出现偏差：其拒绝的多数答案并未被判定为错误，而其接受的部分答案确实存在错误。AI对拒绝内容的人工普查发现，四分之一到三分之一存在语义错误，其余部分主要归因于问题描述不明确、合成实例或疑似参考查询缺陷，这一判断得到了预注册盲审专家审计的支持。预言机还决定了置信度得分的评判标准。在16组组合中，每个执行一致性得分在其构建所用预言机的标签下表现更好；而在专家标签下，基于套件聚类而非原数据库聚类构建得分，在一个检查点上使ROC曲线下面积（AUROC）提升6.96点，在另一个检查点上提升1.53点。在第二个检查点上，专家区间排除了套件标签报告的8.3点提升。因此，证书应同时报告两个预言机的结果，且在基准经过审计前，不应将预言机相对差异解读为语义风险。一致性得分应在未参与其构建的预言机下进行评估。
👤 Jiamiao Liu, Dewen Qiao, Yu Zhang, Xuetao Chen 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Auditing Proxy-Based Validation Across Text Spans
跨文本跨度的代理验证审计
Evaluation scores are often validated by their agreement with inexpensive proxy labels. When the score and the proxy are computed from the same text span, however, that agreement can arise from surface evidence the two share rather than from the semantic construct the proxy is meant to represent. We make the distinction explicit by declaring the score, its span, the proxy and the target construct as a validation contract, then re-evaluating that proxy rule strictly outside the scored span. In a controlled HotpotQA correctness experiment varying only the shared text boundary, the score agrees with its proxy far better than with correctness at a 50-character prefix: the gap is +0.184, collapsing to at most +0.045 from 120 characters onward. At that short prefix the score still predicts whether the answer string appears later (AUC 0.634) while an equivalence test places its agreement with correctness at chance, so the reported proxy agreement does not establish that the score ranks correctness. On OR-Bench, suppressing each model's recurring opening templates removes most of the score's association with the refusal proxy, while matched-volume deletion removes almost none and construct agreement stays at chance. Only three of eleven external contracts support the off-span control, and none of the routing studies we sampled released the generations it needs. We therefore ask that a proxy-based validation claim declare the span each label is read from, report the construct agreement beside the proxy agreement, and release the generations that let the proxy be re-read off the scored span.
中文翻译
评估分数通常通过其与廉价代理标签的一致性来验证。然而，当分数与代理标签来自同一文本跨度时，这种一致性可能源于两者共享的表面证据，而非代理标签本应代表的语义构念。我们通过明确定义分数、其跨度、代理标签和目标构念之间的验证契约，明确区分了这一现象，随后严格在评分跨度之外重新评估该代理规则。在HotpotQA正确性控制实验中，仅改变共享文本边界，分数与代理标签的一致性远高于与正确性的相关性：在50字符前缀处差距为+0.184，从120字符起最多缩小至+0.045。在短前缀阶段，分数仍能预测答案字符串是否后续出现（AUC 0.634），而等价性检验显示其与正确性的相关性处于随机水平，因此报告的代理一致性并不能证明分数能正确排序正确性。在OR-Bench上，抑制模型重复使用的开头模板几乎消除了分数与拒绝代理的相关性，而等量删除则几乎无影响，构念一致性仍处于随机水平。在十一份外部契约中仅有三项支持跨跨度控制，我们采样的路由研究中没有一项公开了生成内容所需的数据。因此，我们呼吁基于代理验证的声明应明确说明每条标签读取的跨度，报告构念一致性与代理一致性的并列结果，并公开允许代理在评分跨度外重新读取的生成内容。
👤 Daein Weon, Dong Ho Kang 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

How Strongly Should Task State Influence an LLM Agent?
任务状态应如何影响LLM智能体？
Long-horizon assigned work requires an LLM agent to track the state of a task: which steps are done, blocked, cancelled, or open to repetition. Agent systems either keep this state as text in the prompt and rely on the model to read that text, or move the state into a module that enforces it, and each system is evaluated as a whole, so no one knows how much reliability comes from the state being shown, told, or enforced. We fix the task rules, the model, and paired episodes and vary how strongly task state reaches the agent: a raw transcript, an exact checklist, per-turn directives from a state machine compiled from the brief and advanced only by execution receipts, or an enforcement gate on that machine that refuses state-violating actions; every episode is scored by exact payload matching against dynamic ground truth. Across three models, two reasoning regimes, and two domains, four findings hold without per-turn reasoning: displaying accurate state is unreliable, an unverified ledger the agent writes itself beats an accurate checklist it is shown, directives help in proportion to the model's obedience, and enforcement needs no obedience but is bounded by the correctness of its state and by the matcher that maps requests to steps; per-turn reasoning at a 235B agent compresses these separations without repairing the text rungs. The same gate, compiled from $\tau^2$-bench's airline policy, raises a 235B agent's pass$^1$ from 0.39 to 0.54 and changes nothing for a 35B agent that rarely violates the policy; on PM-Bench, where acting turns on recognizing a cue rather than on state, showing the record is the best rung--matching or beating both gates and reversing the ledger-over-checklist finding--and enforcing the matcher's judgement drops a 35B agent below its raw transcript. Enforcement pays when failures are state-decidable and frequent, and hurts when the gate's judgement is wrong.
中文翻译
长期任务需要LLM智能体跟踪任务状态：哪些步骤已完成、被阻塞、已取消或可重复执行。当前智能体系统要么将状态作为文本保留在提示中并依赖模型自行读取，要么将其移入一个强制执行状态的模块中，整体系统被评估，因此无法确定可靠性究竟来自状态的展示、告知还是强制。我们固定任务规则、模型和成对的实验场景，改变任务状态传递给智能体的强度：原始转录文本、精确清单、根据简报编译的状态机逐轮指令（仅通过执行确认推进），或对该状态机的强制门控机制，拒绝违反状态的动作；每个实验通过与动态真实值的精确负载匹配进行评分。在三种模型、两种推理模式和两个领域中，四个发现成立且无需逐轮推理：准确显示状态不可靠，智能体自行书写的未经验证账本优于其被展示的准确清单，指令的帮助程度与模型的服从性成正比，而强制机制无需服从性，但受限于其状态的正确性和请求到步骤映射的匹配器；在235B智能体上进行逐轮推理压缩了这些分离，但并未修复文本层级的问题。相同的强制门控机制（基于τ²-bench的航空公司政策）使235B智能体的通过率从0.39提升至0.54，而35B智能体因极少违反政策，结果无变化；在PM-Bench上，行动取决于识别线索而非状态，此时展示记录是最优层级——匹配或超越两种门控机制，并逆转了账本优于清单的发现——而强制匹配器判断则使35B智能体性能低于原始转录文本。当失败是状态决定且频繁发生时，强制机制有效；当门控判断错误时，反而有害。
👤 Chenyu Zhang, Wonbin Kweon, Jiawei Han 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Efficient Cost-Aware LLM Evaluation via Bayesian Bandit Gittins Indices
基于贝叶斯伯努利吉廷斯指数的高效成本感知LLM评估
Exhaustively evaluating every candidate LLM configuration on every benchmark item to identify a high-performing one is costly. We formulate configuration selection as a cost-aware Bayesian bandit problem and propose GittinsEval, which draws on the Bayesian-optimal Gittins policy to determine which configuration to evaluate next and when to stop. We extend the policy with an anytime recommendation rule over both fully and partially evaluated configurations, using an LCB-style score to account for posterior uncertainty. GittinsEval is computationally efficient, requiring only lightweight online updates after offline precomputation. Across GSM8K, PIQA, AlpacaEval, and MMLU response matrices, GittinsEval is consistently competitive, with particularly strong gains over configuration-level Bayesian optimization on large-example benchmarks and over cost-unaware bandit baselines on large-candidate tasks. Crucially, GittinsEval often attains near-zero simple regret using only 1% to 2% of the exhaustive-evaluation cost; it also offers an adaptive stopping rule that typically triggers at 1% to 10%.
中文翻译
对每个候选LLM配置在每个基准项上进行彻底评估以识别高性能配置成本高昂。我们将配置选择建模为成本感知的贝叶斯老虎机问题，并提出GittinsEval，该方法借鉴贝叶斯最优的吉廷斯策略来决定下一步评估哪个配置以及何时停止评估。我们通过一种任意推荐规则扩展该策略，适用于完全和部分评估的配置，采用LCB风格的评分来考虑后验不确定性。GittinsEval计算效率高，仅需在离线预计算后进行轻量级在线更新。在GSM8K、PIQA、AlpacaEval和MMLU响应矩阵上，GittinsEval始终表现优异，尤其在大型示例基准上显著优于配置级贝叶斯优化，在大规模候选任务上也优于无成本意识的老虎机基线。关键的是，GittinsEval通常仅用1%至2%的全评估成本即可达到近零简单遗憾；同时提供自适应停止规则，通常在1%至10%时触发。
👤 Qian Xie, Yueli He, Nairen Cao 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers
训练了却未学习：面向大语言模型智能体作为前向部署工程师的后训练交付基准
Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.
中文翻译
后训练正逐渐成为一种服务（PTaaS）：客户提交数据和目标，前向部署工程师（FDE）在预算、人工审批和可复现性要求下返回微调、评估并部署的模型。将大语言模型智能体置于FDE角色中，现有基准无法回答一个关键问题：智能体是否值得信赖以完成交付？我们在一个受控的交付环境中回答该问题，其中智能体驱动十个阶段，每个阶段由基于平台记录事实的Oracle评分。核心隐性失败是‘训练了但未学习’（TBDL）：损失下降，所有信号保持绿色，但交付模型性能与基础模型无异。操作员运行的验收门禁可在付款前捕获此类运行，而基于已知受损运行校准的检测器可在运行中标识严重损坏。我们在计量的L40S、A100和H200 GPU上对四个前沿智能体（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-luna、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek V4-Pro）在8B至70B规模的开源基础模型上进行了全流程测试，并在评分前对每个场景进行认证。同时，我们还运行了人类FDE对照组，并将每个智能体与之对比。

Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents
Agensh：将组织智能扩展至1,024个智能体
A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.
中文翻译
多智能体系统可通过并发执行任务来降低复杂任务的延迟。尽管已有若干开创性的多智能体框架支持多智能体系统，但当前多智能体框架的可扩展性常受制于中央协调器处理任务分配和工作协调的能力。为解决这一限制，我们引入Agensh，一种无中央协调器的可扩展自组织多智能体框架：并发工作者执行多智能体协作循环，异步地持续收集上下文、申领并自主分配子任务、采取行动、共享发现、验证结果，并合并进展。该循环由智能体组织基础设施支持，包含三个组件：共享工作区存放待定、进行中和已完成的工作；消息接口使工作者之间通信；共享上下文保留可重用的发现和工作意图。为测试Agensh的可扩展性，我们在ProgramBench中最难的五个任务上使用GPT-5.6-sol（高）进行评估。从1个智能体扩展到128个智能体，平均最终测试通过率从19.31%提升至28.78%，相对提升约49%。更大的组织更早达到相近的测试通过率。在pandoc任务上，从1个智能体扩展到1,024个智能体，最终测试通过率从33.89%提升至55.06%。工作者轨迹分析显示，随着组织规模扩大，不同形式的自组织协作逐渐涌现并趋于标准化。这些结果揭示了智能体数量作为一个新的可扩展维度，使多智能体组织能够拓展通用智能的边界，为在严格延迟约束或时间预算下完成复杂任务提供了实用解决方案。
👤 Zhihao Zhan, Ting Song, Li Dong, Shaohan Huang, Jianxun Lian et al. 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue
SpeakerMem-R1：面向多方对话的以说话者为中心的双轨记忆机制
Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.
中文翻译
多方对话场景中的长期对话记忆不仅需要从长期对话中检索相关内容，还必须区分谁说了什么、每条陈述涉及的对象、个体之间的相互认知、群体共享的信息以及状态随时间的变化。近期对多方对话基准的研究表明，现有通用大语言模型的记忆系统往往丢失人物关系或群体关系，难以整合分布在成员、群体和时间中的线索。这些问题共同揭示了两个核心瓶颈：多方对话中的信息归属与关系理解，以及从交错历史中重构状态。为解决上述问题，我们提出$ extbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$：其双轨记忆机制分别存储带说话者标签的原文消息和按个人级与群体级视角组织的推导状态，在查询时通过实体、事件和时间维度融合两轨证据。为在结构化记忆构建过程中减少归属与更新错误，并支持本地部署，我们使用SpeakerLevenshtein和说话者条件GRPO训练Writer-R1。在GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench和EverMemBench上，SpeakerMem-R1分别取得47.9%、69.2%和61.9%的二分类准确率。在EverMind-AI公开发布的EverMemBench排行榜上，我们取得了62.33%的准确率，为最新最先进框架中的最佳结果。此外，在全部1,986个LoCoMo问题上达到70.85%的准确率，我们将其用作两人长期对话的边界测试。在305个问题的受控评估中，强化学习将SFT Writer的平均准确率从57.38%提升至68.20%。我们报告了二分类准确率和token-F1，并通过消融实验表明，原文轨道与结构化轨道，以及个人级与群体级视角在标准化评估界面下具有互补性。
👤 Haobo Zheng, Tan Tang, Yan Chen, Weijie Wang, Yingcai Wu 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv
[2609.26637]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models
强大而精简：提取并表征前沿模型中的隐藏思维链
The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.
中文翻译

Knowledge Pull Requests for Continual Document Authoring
知识拉取请求：持续文档创作的新框架
We introduce Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs), a framework for continual document authoring that makes each change interpretable. Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.
中文翻译
我们提出知识拉取请求（KPRs），一种用于持续文档创作的框架，使每次修改均可解释。文档需要持续修订以整合来自其他来源、语言或时间的新知识，但现有方法要么不记录知识变更内容，要么完全重新生成。KPR通过提取主张、过滤并路由至相应章节、标记与现有内容的冲突，将新知识整合进文档，生成一份ChangeLog，将知识变更（主张提案）与文本变更（文档差异）分离。我们在跨语言维基百科修订和RAGTIME上的查询驱动报告更新中评估KPRs。相比从源重写或从头再生，KPRs能整合更多信息并更好保留原有内容，同时单位生成词元增加的信息量最多。经KPR修订的文章在问答任务中的表现优于使用搜索的前沿模型，后者无法揭示仅存在于其他语言中的知识。

Combining Hierarchical Cognitive Process with Process Supervision for Interpretable Scene Safety Understanding
结合层次化认知过程与过程监督以实现可解释的场景安全理解
Scene safety understanding plays a life-or-death role in situational awareness in various critical domains. Traditional methods that rely on learning direct mappings between scenes and safety levels often lack interpretability, limiting their reliability in critical applications. An effective approach to overcoming this challenge lies in interpreting human cognitive processes and equipping machine models with analogous cognitive capabilities. This work explores an effective way of integrating scene safety cognitive process modeling and process supervision. Specifically, we first construct a hierarchical cognitive safety structure, which motivates the development of a novel, high-quality scene safety understanding dataset based on multi-step reasoning with process labels. This dataset serves both as a benchmark and a resource to improve the safety reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), while also enabling a granular analysis of intermediate reasoning steps through information flow and saliency-based techniques. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a modular and flexible process supervision framework that reflects the hierarchical nature of human cognition. This framework leverages LLMs as the core architecture and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation(LoRA) and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) strategies to enable specialization and collaboration among expert modules, each tasked with specific sub-processes of the overall reasoning chain. Systematic experimental evaluations and analyses confirm that our framework exhibits superior interpretability and performance characteristics compared to traditional approaches.

HySparse2: Hybrid Sparse Attention with Two-Level KV Sharing
HySparse2：两级KV共享的混合稀疏注意力机制
Long-horizon and multi-turn agents typically generate short actions and process long observations from tools and environments. This growing context demands efficient prefill, compact KV-cache storage, and accurate long-context retrieval. To meet these demands, we introduce HySparse2, a hybrid sparse attention architecture with two-level KV sharing. At the outer level, KV Bridging adopts a YOCO-style self-decoder and cross-decoder structure, but bridges only full-attention layers. The self-decoder uses hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA), while the cross-decoder uses hybrid sparse attention. The KV caches for full-attention layers in the cross-decoder are generated from the hidden states of full-attention layers in the self-decoder. At the inner level, HySparse2 retains HySparse's core KV Reuse design with two refinements. First, it replaces block-level sparsity with token-level sparsity for finer long-context retrieval. Second, it removes the separate SWA branch from sparse layers and instead forces a sliding window of recent tokens into the sparse selection. This two-level KV sharing allows all cross-decoder KV caches to be constructed from self-decoder hidden states. Prefill can therefore exit after the self-decoder, skipping all cross-decoder layers. On an 80B-A3B MoE model, HySparse2 outperforms HySparse and Hybrid SWA on long-context retrieval and multi-turn agentic tasks, while substantially reducing prefill computation and KV-cache storage.
中文翻译
长时程和多轮次智能体通常生成短动作并处理来自工具与环境的长观察序列。这种不断增长的上下文需求对高效的预填充、紧凑的KV缓存存储以及准确的长上下文检索提出了挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出HySparse2，一种具有两级KV共享的混合稀疏注意力架构。在外层，KV桥接采用YOCO风格的自解码器与交叉解码器结构，但仅作用于全注意力层。自解码器使用混合滑动窗口注意力（SWA），而交叉解码器使用混合稀疏注意力。交叉解码器中全注意力层的KV缓存由自解码器中全注意力层的隐藏状态生成。在内层，HySparse2保留了HySparse的核心KV重用设计，并进行了两项改进：第一，将块级稀疏性替换为更细粒度的token级稀疏性，以实现更精准的长上下文检索；第二，移除稀疏层中独立的SWA分支，转而强制将最近的token滑动窗口纳入稀疏选择范围。这种两级KV共享机制使得所有交叉解码器的KV缓存均可由自解码器的隐藏状态构建而成，因此预填充可在自解码器完成后退出，跳过所有交叉解码器层。在80B-A3B MoE模型上，HySparse2在长上下文检索和多轮智能体任务中优于HySparse和Hybrid SWA，同时大幅降低预填充计算量和KV缓存存储开销。
👤 Jianyu Wei, Yizhao Gao, Qihao Zhang, Shimao Chen, Zhengju Tang et al. 📅 2026/09/22 🔗 arXiv

Same Chart, Different Story: Bias in Vision-Language Chart Interpretation
同一图表，不同故事：视觉-语言图表解读中的偏见
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret charts and generate natural-language explanations for socially consequential data. However, they may produce different narratives for the same chart when only the referenced social group changes, reinforcing stereotypes and misleading decisions. Despite these risks, no benchmark exists for systematically evaluating bias in chart interpretation across social dimensions. We introduce ChartBias, the first benchmark for auditing bias in VLM-based chart interpretation. ChartBias contains 820 manually curated real-world charts spanning six attributes: race, income, age, religion, immigration status, and gender, yielding 4,319 valid chart, attribute instances and 8,638 paired generations where the chart is fixed and only the group term is swapped. Across 12 proprietary and open-source VLMs, totaling 155,484 model responses, we find three widespread failure modes: narrative shift (same chart, different narratives), group hallucination (assigning a chart to a group without evidence), and preference polarity (favourable trends often linked to one group). We further propose a multi-agent mitigation framework that serves as a strong baseline by separating chart-grounded evidence extraction from group-conditioned generation and using a counterfactual judge to verify that group-driven differences are supported by the chart. The framework substantially reduces narrative shift while preserving chart-grounded reasoning. Our findings show that evaluating chart understanding requires measuring not only accuracy, but also fairness and consistency across social groups. We release ChartBias at this https URL .
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）被广泛用于解释图表并生成自然语言描述，以应对具有社会意义的数据。然而，当仅参考的社会群体发生变化时，它们可能对同一图表生成不同的叙事，从而强化刻板印象并误导决策。尽管存在此类风险，目前尚无系统评估VLM在跨社会维度图表解读中偏见的基准。我们提出了ChartBias，首个用于审计VLM图表解读偏见的基准。ChartBias包含820个手工筛选的真实世界图表，涵盖种族、收入、年龄、宗教、移民身份和性别六个属性，共产生4,319个有效的图表-属性实例和8,638对成对生成内容（图表固定，仅群体术语替换）。在12个专有和开源VLM上，总计155,484条模型响应中，我们发现三种普遍失败模式：叙事转移（相同图表生成不同叙述）、群体幻觉（无证据地将图表归因于某群体）、偏好极性（有利趋势常与某一特定群体关联）。我们进一步提出一种多智能体缓解框架，通过分离基于图表的证据提取与群体条件生成，并利用反事实裁判验证群体驱动差异是否由图表支持，作为强基线。该框架显著减少了叙事转移，同时保持了基于图表的推理能力。研究揭示，评估图表理解不仅需关注准确性，还需衡量跨社会群体的公平性与一致性。我们已在该网址发布ChartBias。

Modality-Gated Deep Adapters: Adding a Modality to a Frozen Embedding Model with Exact Preservation
模态门控深度适配器：在冻结嵌入模型中精确保留输出的前提下添加新模态
Multimodal embedding models are deployed at scale: retrieval indices, benchmark results, and behavioral audits all depend on the base model's exact outputs. Extending such a model to a new modality with existing parameter-efficient methods silently changes those outputs; LoRA-style adaptation rewrites the text path whether or not the weights are merged, invalidating every stored embedding. We propose modality-gated deep adapters: bottleneck adapters attached to every decoder layer of a frozen multimodal embedding LLM, grouped into per-modality packs that execute only while their own modality is being encoded. The result is a modality added with zero change to existing outputs: inputs no pack claims traverse the base model's own computation graph, bit-for-bit unchanged, and co-loaded packs compose with an exact-zero isolation matrix. Both properties are stated as propositions, hold after arbitrary training rather than only at initialization, require no task labels or routing metadata at inference, and are verified by exact-equality tests on the released checkpoints. On one frozen 2B base, the audio pack (injected as connector tokens) improves audio-to-text R@10 by +3.4 to +5.4 points over an identically trained control, positive at every seed and reproduced at eleven times the data; the thermal pack, reusing the base's own frozen vision path, clears its pre-registered acceptance gate roughly sevenfold at every seed and lifts thermal-to-text R@10 from 0.224 to 0.785. An encoder swap locates the missing capacity: an external audio encoder that outranks Whisper-family encoders in CLAP-style comparisons loses by 16 R@10 points inside the frozen LLM, so the capacity belongs in the layers, exactly where the gated adapters place it. We release the audio model, the thermal pack, and the training, evaluation and invariance suites: models at this http URL , code on GitHub.

多模态嵌入模型被大规模部署：检索索引、基准测试结果和行为审计均依赖于基础模型的精确输出。现有的参数高效方法在向模型扩展新模态时会无声地改变这些输出；LoRA 类型的适配会重写文本路径，无论权重是否合并，都会使所有存储的嵌入失效。我们提出模态门控深度适配器：将瓶颈适配器附加到冻结多模态嵌入大语言模型的每一层解码器上，按模态分组，仅在编码对应模态时激活。结果是在不改变现有输出的情况下添加新模态：未被任何适配包覆盖的输入仍沿基础模型自身的计算图运行，逐位不变，且共加载的适配包通过精确零隔离矩阵组合。这两个特性以命题形式陈述，适用于任意训练后而非仅初始化时，推理阶段无需任务标签或路由元数据，并通过发布检查点的精确等式测试验证。在一个冻结的20亿参数基础模型上，音频适配包（通过连接符注入）在音频转文本R@10指标上相比相同训练的对照组提升+3.4至+5.4分，每个种子均为正向提升，且在十一倍数据量下可复现；热成像适配包复用基础模型自身冻结的视觉路径，在每个种子上使预注册接受门限提高约七倍，同时将热成像转文本R@10从0.224提升至0.785。通过编码器替换定位缺失容量：外部音频编码器在CLAP风格比较中优于Whisper家族编码器，但在冻结LLM内部却落后16个R@10点，说明容量应位于模型层内，恰好是门控适配器所放置的位置。我们发布了音频模型、热成像适配包以及训练、评估与不变性套件：模型可在该网址获取，代码在GitHub上。

Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer: A Monotone, Compositional Ordinal Reasoning Head for Large Language Models
可微模糊推理层：面向大语言模型的单调、可组合序数推理头
A state-of-the-art language model asked to interpret "most of most students passed" typically answers "most," though composing two instances of "most" yields a proportion closer to "some." We trace this failure to an architectural choice rather than a data deficit: standard classifier heads treat ordinal categories as independent labels, with no mechanism to respect their natural ordering or compose them algebraically. We introduce the Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer (DFIL), a dual-path prediction head pairing a standard classifier with a scalar-bottlenecked branch grounded in a bank of ordered membership functions. DFIL supplies two structural primitives that a label-only head cannot inherit: monotonicity in the underlying quantity, and compositional reasoning via t-norm operations without any compositional training data. The scalar branch additionally provides an interpretable interface for analyzing residual errors. We instantiate DFIL on ordinal natural-language tasks across diverse LLM families.
